None and observed > max_sec:\n                    msg = f\"duration {observed:.3f}s exceeds max_sec {max_sec}\"\n                    return False, msg, details, _make_failure(\n                        ctx,\n                        name=\"duration\",\n                        expected={\"max_sec\": max_sec},\n                        actual=observed,\n                        message=msg,\n                    )\n\n                if min_sec is not None and observed < min_sec:\n                    msg = f\"duration {observed:.3f}s below min_sec {min_sec}\"\n                    return False, msg, details, _make_failure(\n                        ctx,\n                        name=\"duration\",\n                        expected={\"min_sec\": min_sec},\n                        actual=observed,\n                        message=msg,\n                    )\n\n                if max_sec is None and min_sec is None:\n                    return True, f\"duration={observed:.3f}s (informational)\", _named_callable(\"duration\", _run)\n\n    # ---- Declarative form -------------------------------------------------\n\n    @classmethod\n    def from_mapping(cls, mapping: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ExpectCallable:\n        \"\"\"Resolve a YAML ``expects:`` row to an :data:`ExpectCallable`.\n\n        The mapping must have exactly one key, matching one of\n        :data:`PRIMITIVE_NAMES`. The associated value is forwarded as\n        keyword arguments to the primitive::\n\n            {\"exit_code\": {\"equals\": 0}}\n            -> Expect.exit_code(equals=0)\n\n        Empty value              \"Expect.from_mapping requires exactly one primitive key; \"\n                f\"got {sorted(mapping)!r}\"\n            )\n        (key, value), = mapping.items()\n        if key not in Mapping[str, Any] = value if isinstance(value, Mapping) else {}\n        primitive = getattr(cls, key)\n        return primitive(**kwargs)\n\n\ndef _stream_predicate(\n    *,\n    name: str,\n :meth:`Expect.stdout` / :meth:`Expect.stderr`.\"\"\"\n\n    compiled = re.compile(regex) if regex is not None else None\n\n    def _run(ctx: EvalContext) -> ExpectResult:\n        def _build() -> tuple[bool, str, Mapping[str, Any], Optional[ExpectFailure]]:\n            buf = _strip_ansi(source(ctx))\n\n            if contains is not None and contains not in buf:\n                msg = f\"{name} missing substring {contains!r}\"\n                return False, msg, {\"stream\": name}, _make_failure(\n                    ctx,\n                    name=name,\n                    expected={\"contains\": contains},\n                    actual=buf,\n                    message=msg,\n                )\n\n            if compiled is not None and compiled.search(buf) is None:\n        expected={\"regex\": regex},\n                    actual=buf,\n                    message=msg,\n                )\n\n            if not_contains is not None and not_contains in buf:\n              expected={\"not_contains\": not_contains},\n                    actual=buf,\n                    message=msg,\n                )\n\n            return True, f\"{name} predicates \"status\": \"allow\",\n        \"ts\": 1,\n    },\n    {\n        \"event\": \"PostToolUse\",\n        \"matcher\": \"mcp__auggie__*\",\n        \"tool\": \"mcp__auggie__codebase-retrieval\",\n        \"status\": \"ok\",\n        \"ts\": 2,\n    },\n    {\n        \"event\": \"PreToolUse\",\n        \"matcher\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__*\",\n        \"tool\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__list\",\n        \"status\": \"allow\",\n        \"ts\": 3,\n    },\n    {\n        \"event\": \"PostToolUse\",\n        \"matcher\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__*\",\n        \"tool\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__list\",\n        \"status\": \"ok\",\n        \"ts\": 4,\n    },\n    {\n        \"event\": \"Stop\",\n        \"matcher\": None,\n        \"tool\": None,\n        \"status\": \"session_end\",\n        \"ts\": 5,\n    },\n)\n\n\ndef _write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Serialize ``rows`` to ``path`` as one JSON object per line (UTF-8).\"\"\"\n    payload = \"\".join(json.dumps(r) + \"\\n\" for r in rows)\n    path.write_text(payload, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef hook_log(home: HomeIsolation) -> Path:\n    return _write_jsonl(home.home_path / \"hooks.jsonl\", HOOK_ROWS)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# path resolution\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_named_path_resolves_via_jsonl_paths(\n    eval_spec: EvalSpec, home: HomeIsolation, hook_log: Path, tmp_path: Path\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"``path`` first resolves against ``ctx.jsonl_paths`` by name.\"\"\"\n    ctx = _make_ctx(\n        eval_spec=eval_spec,\n        home=home,\n        run_dir=tmp_path,\n test_absolute_path_is_used_verbatim(\n    eval_spec: EvalSpec, home: HomeIsolation, tmp_path: Path\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Absolute ``path`` bypasses home_path entirely.\"\"\"\n    target = tmp_path / Returns\nan `ExpectCallable = Callable[[EvalContext], ExpectResult]` (DM-009).\n\n## Argument semantics\n\n| Arg | Type | When set | Pass condition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `path` | `str | Path` | Always required | First looked up in `ctx.jsonl_paths` (named registry — runner-provided). Falls back to `_resolve_path(ctx, path)`: relative paths resolve against `ctx.home_path` (DM-006); absolute paths used verbatim. |\n| `line_count` | `Optional[int]` | Exact row count | The **filtered** row set has exactly this many entries. Blank / whitespace-only lines are skipped at parse time so a trailing newline is not counted. |\n| `filter` | `Optional[predicate]` | Row narrowing | When set, `filtered = [r for r in rows if filter(r)]`; all subsequent assertions evaluate against this subset. |\n| `assert_each` | `Optional[predicate]` | ∀ rows | Every row in the filtered set must return truthy from the predicate. Short-circuits on the first failing row. |\n| `assert_any` | `Optional[predicate]` | ∃ row | At least one row in the filtered set must return truthy. Failure when the filtered set is empty or no row matches. |\n\nEvaluation order inside the primitive:\n\n1. Resolve `path` (named lookup → filesystem fallback).\n2. FAIL early if the path does not exist (`expected={\"exists\": True}` payload).\n3. Parse the file line-by-line, skipping blank lines. Invalid JSON on\n   any line yields a structured FAIL (`expected=\"valid JSON per line\"`,\n   `actual=<raw line>`, message includes the line number).\n4. Apply `filter` (or accept all rows when unset).\n5. Check `line_count` (filtered length).\n6. Check `assert_each` (short-circuits on first miss; records\n   `details[\"row_index\"]` and the offending row in `failure.actual`).\n7. Check `assert_any` (FAIL if no row satisfies the predicate).\n\nThe first failing check produces the `ExpectResult.failure` (DM-005); the\nremaining checks are not evaluated.\n\n## Predicate signatures\n\nAll three predicates share the same shape:\n\n```python\npredicate: Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], bool]\n```\n\nEach row is the deserialised JSON object for that line; primitive\ncallers must accept a mapping (not a class) so the same predicate works\nfor manifest-loaded rows (`dict`) and runner-emitted rows (potentially\n`MappingProxyType`). Predicates are invoked under the same exception\ncontract as the rest of the primitive: an uncaught exception bubbles\nout and is caught one level up by the runner's ExpectCallable harness\n(T03.04), which records the failure with a traceback.\n\n## Failure payload by branch\n\n| Branch | `expected` | `actual` | `message` shape |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Missing file | `{\"exists\": True}` | `{\"exists\": False}` | `\"jsonl path <abs> does not exist\"` |\n| Invalid JSON line | `\"valid JSON per line\"` | `<raw line text>` | `\"line <N> not valid JSON: <decoder error>\"` (with `traceback` populated) |\n| `line_count` mismatch | `{\"line_count\": <expected>}` | `{\"line_count\": <observed>}` | `\"line_count expected <X> got <Y>\"` |\n| `assert_each` fail | `{\"assert_each\": \"predicate true\"}` | `<offending row mapping>` | `\"assert_each failed at index <N>\"`; `details[\"row_index\"] = N` |\n| `assert_any` empty | `{\"assert_any\": \"any matching row\"}` | `[<all filtered rows>]` | `\"assert_any predicate satisfied by no row\"` |\n\nOn the PASS path, `details` carries\n`{\"path\": <resolved abs>, \"rows_inspected\": <len(filtered)>}` so the\nReporter can render the row count it actually exercised even when no\npredicate was set.\n\n## Path resolution invariants\n\n* The named lookup (`ctx.jsonl_paths[path_str]`) takes precedence. This\n  is how the runner advertises per-eval log files (`hook_log`,\n  `claude_session_log`) without manifest authors having to know the\n  on-disk path under the scratch HOME.\n* When `path` is not a registered name, it falls back to filesystem\n  resolution: absolute paths used verbatim; relative paths joined\n  against `ctx.home_path` (DM-006).\n* Resolution happens at *callable invocation time*, not construction,\n  so one primitive can run against many EvalContexts (one per eval\n  invocation).\n\n## Hook-telemetry shape used by the test matrix\n\nThe acceptance test fixture mirrors the per-eval `hooks.jsonl` written\nby HookAdapter (T02.16) / EvalRunner (T03.06):\n\n```jsonl\n{\"event\": \"PreToolUse\",  \"matcher\": \"mcp__auggie__*\",            \"tool\": \"mcp__auggie__codebase-retrieval\", \"status\": \"allow\",       \"ts\": 1}\n{\"event\": \"PostToolUse\", \"matcher\": \"mcp__auggie__*\",            \"tool\": \"mcp__auggie__codebase-retrieval\", \"status\": \"ok\",          \"ts\": 2}\n{\"event\": \"PreToolUse\",  \"matcher\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__*\", \"tool\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__list\",    \"status\": \"allow\",       \"ts\": 3}\n{\"event\": \"PostToolUse\", \"matcher\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__*\", \"tool\": \"mcp__airis-mcp-gateway__list\",    \"status\": \"ok\",          \"ts\": 4}\n{\"event\": \"Stop\",        \"matcher\": null,                        \"tool\": null,                              \"status\": \"session_end\", \"ts\": 5}\n```\n\nE1 (sticky-lifecycle, T05.02) and E2.x (matcher-coverage, T05.10) use\nthis exact shape with `Expect.jsonl` to assert hook invariants.\n\n## Test matrix (`tests/cli/eval/test_expect_jsonl.py`)\n\n| # | Test | Argument under test | Direction |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `test_named_path_resolves_via_jsonl_paths` | `path` (named lookup) | PASS |\n| 2 | `test_relative_path_resolves_under_home` | `path` (relative fallback) | PASS |\n| 3 | `test_absolute_path_is_used_verbatim` | `path` (absolute) | PASS |\n| 4 | `test_missing_file_fails_with_existence_payload` | `path` (missing) | FAIL |\n| 5 | `test_line_count_passes_on_exact_match` | `line_count` | PASS |\n| 6 | `test_line_count_fails_on_mismatch` | `line_count` | FAIL |\n| 7 | `test_line_count_zero_passes_on_empty_jsonl` | `line_count=0` | PASS |\n| 8 | `test_line_count_ignores_blank_lines` | parser robustness | PASS |\n| 9 | `test_filter_narrows_rows_before_line_count` | `filter` + `line_count` | PASS |\n| 10 | `test_filter_to_zero_rows_passes_with_count_zero` | `filter` (empty result) | PASS |\n| 11 | `test_assert_each_passes_when_all_rows_match` | `assert_each` (∀) | PASS |\n| 12 | `test_assert_each_fails_on_first_mismatch` | `assert_each` (∀) | FAIL |\n| 13 | `test_assert_any_passes_when_one_row_matches` | `assert_any` (∃) | PASS |\n| 14 | `test_assert_any_fails_when_no_row_matches` | `assert_any` (∃) | FAIL |\n| 15 | `test_assert_any_runs_against_filtered_subset` | `filter` + `assert_any` | FAIL |\n| 16 | `test_invalid_json_line_fails_with_lineno` | parser error path | FAIL |\n| 17 | `test_no_assertions_passes_when_file_parsable` | all-None | PASS |\n| 18 | `test_combined_filter_line_count_assert_each` | combined | PASS |\n| 19 | `test_result_carries_primitive_name_and_timing` | result envelope | PASS |\n\nAll 19 cases pass under `uv run pytest tests/cli/eval/test_expect_jsonl.py -v`\nin 0.15 s on 2026-05-20.\n\n## Downstream consumers\n\n* T05.02 E1 sticky-lifecycle eval — asserts the hook JSONL contains a\n  matching PreToolUse / PostToolUse pair per `mcp__auggie__*` invocation\n  via matcher pattern.\n\n## Linked roadmap entries\n\n* R-066 — COMP-010.2 / D-0066\n* Depends on: T04.01 (Expect package skeleton)\n* Used by: T05.02 (E1), T05.10 {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01JuwfwYv9DfJVicQatBZ9uB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013bmz4JgP6rNqJ4A8zUsRDg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0066/notes.md","content":"# D-0066 — Implementation notes\n\n## Why this task was test-only\n\nT04.01 (D-0064) shipped the full `Expect.jsonl` body alongside the\npackage skeleton: named-path lookup via `ctx.jsonl_paths`, blank-line-\ntolerant parsing, per-line JSON decode with line-numbered error\nreporting, filter → line_count → assert_each → assert_any evaluation\norder, and structured `ExpectFailure` records for each failure branch.\nT04.03's deliverable is the **per-primitive acceptance harness** that\ntouches every named argument and the predicate semantics under\nhook-telemetry fixture inputs. No body changes were required to\n`src/superclaude/cli/eval/expect.py`.\n\n## Decisions\n\n1. **Hook-telemetry shape is the canonical fixture.** The test module\n   defines a single `HOOK_ROWS` tuple mirroring the per-eval\n   `hooks.jsonl` HookAdapter writes (T02.16). Every assertion test\n   exercises one of: matcher-pattern selection, status equality, event\n   sequence, session-stop marker. This pins the fixture shape that E1\n   (T05.02) and E2.x (T05.10) will rely on, so any future schema\n   change to the runner's emitted JSONL must update the fixture and\n   every downstream eval together.\n2. **Predicate signature is `Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], bool]`.**\n   Manifest-loaded rows come back from `json.loads` as plain dicts; the\n   primitive type-hints the predicate against `Mapping` so callers can\n   wrap rows in `MappingProxyType` without breaking. The test for\n   `assert_each` and `assert_any` uses bare `dict.get` to confirm the\n   common case.\n3. **`line_count` counts the *filtered* set, not the raw row count.**\n   `test_filter_narrows_rows_before_line_count` and\n   `test_filter_to_zero_rows_passes_with_count_zero` pin this ordering;\n   it is the only ordering that lets manifests assert \"exactly 2\n   PreToolUse rows\" without a follow-up `filter` argument on the\n   `line_count` itself.\n4. **Blank lines are skipped silently.** Hook adapters sometimes flush\n   buffered lines with a trailing newline; counting that newline as a\n   row would force every eval to set `line_count=N+1`. The test\n   `test_line_count_ignores_blank_lines` documents the behaviour.\n5. **Invalid JSON yields a structured FAIL, not an uncaught\n   exception.** `test_invalid_json_line_fails_with_lineno` asserts the\n   line number is included in the message and the offending raw text\n   is in `failure.actual`. The traceback is also populated so the\n   Reporter can surface the `json.JSONDecodeError` in verbose output.\n6. **Path resolution at call time.** Resolution lives inside `_run`,\n   not at primitive construction, so one manifest-built callable runs\n   across many EvalContexts. The named-lookup branch is hit first;\n   `test_named_path_resolves_via_jsonl_paths` and the two `path`-string\n   tests pin both branches.\n\n## Things explicitly not covered here\n\n* **Predicate exceptions raising mid-iteration** — not exercised. The\n  primitive deliberately lets predicate exceptions propagate to the\n  runner's ExpectCallable harness (T03.04), which wraps them with a\n  traceback. Catching them inside `Expect.jsonl` would mask predicate\n  bugs (e.g. typos in `row.get(\"eventt\")`) as silent FAILs without\n  context. The contract is \"predicate must not raise\"; misuse is a\n  manifest authoring error, not a primitive concern.\n* **Non-mapping JSON values** — a JSONL of bare integers (`5\\n6\\n`)\n  would parse cleanly and pass the `assert_each`/`assert_any`\n  predicates with whatever the predicate does on a non-mapping input.\n  This is intentional: the primitive doesn't enforce row shape; the\n  predicate does.\n* **Mutually-exclusive argument validation** — none. Unlike\n  `Expect.exit_code` (`equals` vs `in_set`), every argument here is\n  composable. The test matrix covers the orthogonal combinations.\n\n## Follow-ups\n\nNone. Two adjacent tasks consume this primitive:\n\n* T05.02 (E1) wires `Expect.jsonl` into the sticky-lifecycle eval,\n  asserting the post-stop JSONL row count and matcher coverage.\n* T05.10 (E2.x) wires it into per-matcher coverage assertions\n  validating that every declared matcher pattern emits at least one\n  PreToolUse row.\n\nBoth pass without any further changes to `Expect.jsonl`.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3714,"cache_read_input_tokens":136838,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3714,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":46,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6ce8e75-9b06-4f89-88e3-3f9040b6551e","uuid":"e8af706c-f59b-4a41-8828-10b249697375"}
